A) Subgradient multiplicative update (SGPMU):
Given p normalized. Compute c, max M. Active weights w_k = exp(β(c_k - M)) / count, or just threshold c_k > M - δ. Then gradient d_i = 2 * correlation(w, p_i? This gradient approximates subgradient of soft max. Then update θ_i -= η d_i, p_i = exp(θ_i - LSE(θ)). This can lower max. Step size η tune.

Implementation detail with FFT convolution:
```
def cross_lag(p, alpha):
    n=len(p)
    pr = p[::-1]
    size=1 << ((2*n-1 + n-1 -1).bit_length())? conv length len(alpha)+n-1 = (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-2 (if alpha length 2n-1). Need size >= 3n-2.
    fa=fft(alpha,size); fb=fft(pr,size); conv=irfft(fa*fb)[:3*n-2]
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n
```
Check indices: conv[m]=Σ_s alpha_s pr_{m-s}. Take m=i+n-1. s=i+j, pr_{n-1-j}=p_j. Good.
Could also compute using np.correlate(alpha, p, mode='valid') for small n; but FFT okay.
- `soft_max_weights(c, beta)`: m=c.max(); logw=beta*(c-m); logsum=np.log(np.sum(np.exp(logw))); return np.exp(logw-logsum). For performance, can threshold if beta large: only active. Use beta maybe 1000-5000? Need stable.

    p = p0.copy(); normalize
    theta = np.log(p+1e-300)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = score(p)
    beta = beta_start
    lr = 0.1
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time expired: break
       p = exp(theta - logsumexp(theta)); maybe if not renormalize due to numerical
       c = conv_self(p)
       weights = soft_weights(c,beta)
       cross = cross_lag(p, weights)
       grad = 2*n*cross - 2.0  # center mean approx 2
       # optional gradient noise
       theta -= lr * grad
       # clip theta? to prevent under/overflow: theta = np.clip(theta, theta.mean()-50, theta.mean()+50)

       cand = exp(theta - logsumexp(theta))
       s = score(cand)
       if s < best: best=cand; maybe if no improvement over many, reduce beta or restart.
```

Full-sequence local descent implementation details:
We need choose initial p from Fourier best or multi start. Let's create a list of candidates to run descent. Time allocation:
- Fourier optimization: maybe 200-400s.
- Main descent: from best Fourier, use remaining 1000s.
- Additional restarts: if time left, start from random shapes and short descents.
But propose_candidate can run until deadline, returning best. We'll code while time<deadline.

Let's propose a primary descent algorithm `subgradient_descent(p0, time_limit, max_iter?)` returning best p/score.
Pseudo code:
```
def subgradient_descent(p0, time_limit=600, delta_start=0.15, delta_stop=0.01, step=0.35):
   n=len(p0); p=normalize(p0.copy())
   best=p; best_score=eval(p)
   c=conv_self(p)
   theta=get_theta(p)
   beta? use active
   delta=delta_start
   no_improve=0
   step_cur=step
   while time.monotonic()<time_limit:
       c=conv_self(p)  # maybe already have c from last; manage
       M=c.max()
       thresh = M - delta*M
       active = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
       if len(active)==0: active=[int(np.argmax(c))]
       w = 1.0/len(active)
       d = active_cross(p, active, w)  # returns cross = Σ p_j w_{i+j}
       grad = 2.0 * n * d
       grad -= grad.mean()
       # maybe add small noise to escape: grad += noise_std * rng.normal(size=n)
       theta -= step_cur * grad
       theta = stable_normalize_theta(theta)
       p_new = np.exp(theta); p_new/=p_new.sum()
       c_new = conv_self(p_new)
       score_new = 2*n*c_new.max()/1.0 # since sum1
       if score_new < best_score:
           best=p_new; best_score=score_new; no_improve=0
           # increase step slightly
           step_cur = min(step_max, step_cur*1.02)
           # reduce delta? maybe if improved, reduce active width? If rely.
           delta = max(delta_stop, delta*0.995)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           # if worsened too much, revert
           if score_new > best_score * 1.05:
                step_cur = max(step_min, step_cur*0.7)
                p=p; c=c # revert? We already changed p/theta; need keep old
                # we can keep old theta/p
           else:
                p=p_new; c=c_new # accept small worsening? For annealing.
                if no_improve > patience: restore best? maybe
   return best,best_score
```

FFT cross:
```
def fft_cross(alpha, p, size):
    n=len(p)
    pr = p[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(alpha, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(pr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, size)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need alpha length 2n-1. If using uniform active, we can avoid building dense alpha? For FFT, dense needed. If active many, okay.
But note alpha sum=1; p sum=1; cross sum=1. `2*n*d` mean=2. Good.

Self conv:
```
def self_conv(a, size=None):
   n=len(a)
   if size is None: size=1; while size<2*n-1: size<<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
```

Objective for BFGS:
```
def obj_grad(x, n, beta):
   logits=np.empty(n); logits[:n-1]=x; logits[n-1]=0.0
   # stable softmax
   logits -= logits.max()
   p=np.exp(logits); p/=p.sum()
   c=convolution(p)
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   M=c.max()
   if M <=0: return 1e300, np.zeros(n-1)
   beta_eff = beta
   logw=beta_eff*(c-M)
   # avoid underflow
   logw = np.clip(logw, -50, 0)
   s=np.exp(logw).sum()
   if s==0: w = np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1
   else: w=np.exp(logw); w/=w.sum()
   # LSE value: M + (1/beta)*logS where logS = beta*(c-M) clipped? If clipped, use unclipped logsum? For objective only for optimization; clipping may perturb. We can compute using stable without clip or use logsumexp manually.
```
Need accurate LSE for optimizer. Use `logsumexp`: mmax? We already shift by M. `log_s = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(logw)))` with logw maybe down to -745; underflow to 0 for below -700 fine. But if all underflow? at least max has logw=0 exp1, s>=1. Good. No clip needed except optional for speed; we can just do exp(logw). Underflow okay. `F = M + np.log(s)/beta`. F, g = objective(x,beta)
step = initial (e.g. 0.1)
for iter:
   if timeout: break
   for ls in range(max_ls):
       x_trial=x - step*g
       F_trial, _=objective(x_trial,beta)
       if F_trial < F:
           x=x_trial; F=F_trial; g=grad; break
       step *= 0.5
   if no accept: step=0.1; maybe random perturbation
```

Detailed code architecture:
Global variables for deadline and objective state:
```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False

class _TimeoutException(Exception): pass
_SEARCH_DEADLINE = 0.0
_N_OBJ = 0
_BETA_OBJ = 1.0

def _raise_if_deadline():
    if time.monotonic() >= _SEARCH_DEADLINE - 0.5:
        raise _TimeoutException()

def _softmax_from_x(x, n):
    logits = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0])
    logits[:-1] = x
    logits[-1] = 0.0
    logits = logits - np.max(logits)
    # clip to avoid under/overflow
    logits = np.clip(logits, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(logits)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <=0: p[:] = 1.0/n
    else: p /= s
    return p
```
For variable x, if last logit fixed 0, gauges; x floats.
Functions for convolution:
```
def _conv_self(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n <= 200:
       return np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
   size=1
   while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1].real
```
For gradients small n, use direct cross:
```
def _cross_direct(p, w):
   n=len(p); m=2*n-1
   d=np.zeros(n)
   # if w dense, direct loops over k with nonzero w. For n<=200, double loop can be optimized using slices.
   for k, wk in enumerate(w):
       if wk == 0.0: continue
       i_start = max(0, k - (n-1))
       i_end = min(n-1, k)
       if i_start > i_end: continue
       start = k - i_start
       stop = k - i_end
       if start == stop:
           d[i_start] += wk * p[start]
       else:
           # p indices decrease from start to stop
           # Need ensure slice covers exactly i_end-i_start+1
           d[i_start:i_end+1] += wk * p[start:stop-1:-1]
   return d
```
Check slice when start>stop: `p[start:stop-1:-1]` includes start down to stop. If stop=0, stop-1=-1, slice `p[start:-1:-1]` excludes index0! Bug. For stop=0, need `p[start::-1]`. Rule: `stop_slice = stop - 1` but if stop == 0, use None. Simpler:
```
indices = start, start-1, ..., stop
if start == stop: one
elif stop == 0: d[...] = wk*p[start::-1]
else: d[...] = wk*p[start:stop-1:-1]
```
Test k=0: start=0, stop=0 -> p[0]. k=n-1? i_start=0,i_end=n-1? start=n-1, stop=0 -> p[n-1::-1] correct. k=1: i_start=0,i_end=1, start=1, stop=0 -> p[1::-1] [p1,p0] correct. k=2,n>2: start=2,stop=0. Good. k=2,n=3? n-1=2, i_start=0? k=2 <=n-1? start=2, stop=0 -> p[2::-1]. c_2 pairs i0j2, i1j1, i2j0; d0 += p2, d1 +=p1,d2+=p0. correct. If k> n-1, e.g., n=4,k=5: i_start=2,i_end=3, start=3, stop=2 -> p[3:1:-1] gives [3,2] correct (stop-1=1). Good.
```
For small n, this is fine.
```
For large n direct threshold maybe 128; for BFGS small n ok.

Smooth objective wrapper:
```
def _smooth_obj_grad(x):
    n = _N_OBJ; beta = _BETA_OBJ
    _raise_if_deadline()
    p = _softmax_from_x(x,n)
    c = _conv_self(p)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    S = p.sum() # 1
    # If using nonnegative? yes
    M = c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(len(x))
    beta = max(1.0, beta)
    # log weights shifted by M
    zw = beta * (c - M)
    # underflow safe
    expw = np.exp(zw)  # values <=1
    s = expw.sum()
    if s <= 0: # should not
       w = np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
       w = expw / s
    G = float(np.dot(w, c))
    cross = _cross_direct(p, w)  # sum=w? w sum1, p sum1 => cross sum1
    v = 2.0 * cross
    grad_full = p * v - (2.0 * G) * p
    # For parameter x, gradient for first n-1
    grad = grad_full[:-1]
    # Stable LSE value: M + log(s)/beta (since shifted by M)
    F = M + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    # Add tiny penalty if p has zeros? not needed.
    # Optionally add exact score mismatch? no.
    return F, grad
```
But `np.exp(zw)` with beta maybe 1000 and zw -1000 underflows; okay. If beta*(c-M) for c-M maybe positive due floating? Should not but could if M from max and c has tiny >M? no. Clip to 0: `zw = np.minimum(0.0, beta*(c-M))`.
The LSE value F: If beta large, log(s) around 0 for active few, F approx M. Good.
Gradient scale: v=2cross average 2, grad components O(p) ~1/n? Actually grad sum zero. For n=3, p~0.1-0.4, v~2, grad~? Fine. In gradient descent, step choice.

Exact objective wrapper:
```
def _exact_obj_x(x):
   n=_N_OBJ; _raise_if_deadline()
   p=_softmax_from_x(x,n)
   c=_conv_self(p); S=p.sum()
   score=2*n*float(np.max(np.maximum(c,0)))/float(S*S)
   if not np.isfinite(score): return 1e100
   return score
```
Maybe add small LSE for continuity? Nelder handles exact.

```
def _x_from_p(p):
   n=len(p)
   if n==1: return np.array([], dtype=float)
   denom=max(p[-1],1e-12)
   x=np.log(p[:-1]/denom)
   return np.clip(x, -15, 15)
```

                # stage Nelder exact

Implementation of `_score(p)`:
```
def _score(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   S=p.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return np.inf
   c=_conv_self(p/S) # normalized sum1 maybe
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if not finite return inf
   return 2*len(p)*M
```
Since if normalize sum1, sum^2=1. For exact evaluate with original scale, same. Use normalized for internal.
But for returning sequence, p sum1 okay. S=1.

Seeds generation:
```
def _seed_p(n, idx, rng):
   if n==1: return np.ones(1)
   kind = idx % 6
   if kind==0: p=np.ones(n)/n
   elif kind==1:
      x=np.arange(n)/n
      amp=0.7
      p=(1+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x))/n
      # if any <=0 due amp, clip
   elif kind==2: amp=0.5 tanh
      p=(1+amp*np.tanh(2.0*(x-0.5)))/n
   elif kind==3: p=rng.dirichlet(alpha=0.3*np.ones(n)); # but dirichlet with alpha small may zeros? no
   elif kind==4: p=rng.dirichlet(alpha=1.0*np.ones(n))
   elif kind==5: p=rng.dirichlet(alpha=2.0*np.ones(n))
   if p.sum()<1e-9: p=np.ones(n)/n
   return p/p.sum()
```
For small n, sine with amp 0.7 gives positive at endpoints 1, min .3; normalized.

def _known_small_p(n):

    if n==5: maybe [5,1,3,1,5]? Need score? Let's compute? Maybe not.
```

```
def _optimize_small_n(n, x0, max_bfgs_iters=100, max_nm_iters=200, use_nm=True):
    p0 = _softmax_from_x(x0,n); s0 = _score(p0)
    best_x = x0.copy(); best_score=s0
    bounds = [(-6.0,6.0)]*(n-1)
    x = best_x.copy()
    for beta in (80.0, 400.0):
       if time.monotonic() >= _SEARCH_DEADLINE: break
       _N_OBJ=n; _BETA_OBJ=beta
       try:
          res = minimize(_smooth_obj_grad, x, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': max_bfgs_iters, 'ftol':1e-9})
          if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
             x2 = np.asarray(res.x)
             p2 = _softmax_from_x(x2,n); sc2 = _score(p2)
             if sc2 < best_score:
                 best_score=sc2; best_x=x2; x=x2
       except _TimeoutException: break
       except Exception: pass
    if use_nm and n <= 20:
       _N_OBJ=n
       for x_start in (best_x, x0):
          # maybe round/clip
          try:
             res = minimize(_exact_obj_x, x_start, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter': max_nm_iters, 'xatol':1e-7, 'fatol':1e-10})
             if hasattr(res,'x'):
                p2=...
                if sc2<best_score: update
          except ...
    return best_p, best_score, best_x
```

Top-level constants and helper code outline:
```
import time
import numpy as np
import sys
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False

class _TimeLimitException(Exception):
    pass

_SEARCH_DEADLINE = 0.0
_N_OBJ = 0
_BETA_OBJ = 100.0

def _timeout():
   if time.monotonic() >= _SEARCH_DEADLINE - 2.0:
       raise _TimeLimitException()

def _conv_self(a): ...
def _score(p): ...
def _softmax_from_x(x,n): ...
def _x_from_p(p): ...
def _cross_direct(p,w): ...
def _smooth_obj_grad(x): ...
def _exact_obj_x(x): ...
def _known_p(n): ...
def _seed_p(n, idx, rng): ...
def _optimize_small_n(n, x0, use_nm): ...
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs): ...
```
No lambda. Top-level okay.

Need be careful with global variables and multiple objective calls: `_smooth_obj_grad` uses `_N_OBJ` and `_BETA_OBJ`. If `minimize` calls objective after we've set for next? No, sequential. But if exception occurs and `minimize` retries? No.

`_smooth_obj_grad` must return gradient array length `len(x)`. If `n=1`, x empty but not called. For `n` global, if x length mismatched? should match n-1.
Stable LSE:
```
z = beta * (c - M)
z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -np.inf)
z = np.minimum(0.0, z)
exp_z = np.exp(z)
s = exp_z.sum()
if s <= 0: # should not
   w = np.zeros_like(c); w[argmax]=1
else: w=exp_z/s
F = M + np.log(s)/beta
```
If c has negative tiny, M maybe small. F positive.
Gradient: `cross = _cross_direct(p,w)`. Since `w` sum1. `v=2*cross`. `G=np.dot(w,c)`. `grad_full=p*v - 2*G*p`. This is gradient wrt all logit variables. But for softmax with last fixed, derivative wrt x = grad_full[:-1]? Yes because the average direction is flat; full gradient sum zero. With parameter x (first n-1, last=0), the gradient is indeed components 0..n-2. Let's verify with simple: If full logit vector l=(x,0), p. The derivative wrt x_k for k<n-1 is full gradient component k (since last logit constant). Good.
But L-BFGS-B bounds on x; full gradient components in bounds. Good.
Check object scalar F: If beta changes, gradient as sum weights not scaled. Suppose F = M + logS/β. Derivative: M' = Σw c_i = G; (logS/β)'? logS = Σ e^{β(c-M)}? Since β constant in optimization, derivative of logS/β = (1/β)*(1/logS)*Σ e^{β(c-M)} β ∂(c-M) = Σw ∂c. Wait derivative of M? Let's compute F = (1/β) log Σ e^{βc}. Using shift: = M + (1/β) log Σ e^{β(c-M)}. Derivative = ∂M + (1/β)*(1/S)*Σ e^{β(c-M)} β∂(c-M). ∂M = Σ (c_i-max?)? Since max not differentiable. But standard LSE derivative is Σw ∂c, where w=e^{βc}/Σe^{βc}. No extra ∂M. Let's rederive with unshifted: F=(1/β) log S, S=Σe^{βc}. ∂F=(1/β)*(1/S)*Σ e^{βc}β∂c=Σw∂c. Using shifted S'=Σe^{β(c-M)}, w=e^{β(c-M)}/S'. F=M+(1/β)log S'. ∂M + (1/β)*(1/S')*Σe^{β(c-M)}β∂(c-M)=∂M + Σw(∂c-∂M)=Σw∂c. Good. So gradient correct.
Our G = Σw c. ∂G wrt logit = J^T(2cross)? Actually G=Σw c with fixed w, derivative = J^T ∂G/∂p = p... yes. Since ∂F=∂G (weights fixed) for F at point? Yes standard LSE derivative equals derivative of weighted sum at current weights. Good.
Thus gradient `grad_full = J^T v` with v=∂G/∂p =2cross. Our formula p*v - p*(p·v) with p·v=2G. Good.

However, if softmax x has last fixed 0, the Jacobian from x to p is not full J but projection; gradient wrt x = full grad components for i<n-1 ONLY if full grad sum zero. Our grad_full sum? p·v=2G, sum grad_full = p·v - 2G*sum p =2G-2G=0. Good. So components correct.

One subtlety: In `p*v - 2G*p`, `p*v` is elementwise, `2G*p`. Good.

Now exact objective `_exact_obj_x` returns score of p. Since p sum1, score=2nM. It doesn't include sum; if p normalized to 1 exactly. Use `c = _conv_self(p)`. If tiny negative max, use max(0). If S not 1 due numeric, normalize or use S: score=2*n*max(c)/S*S. Do that.

`_score(p)` similar.

Direct cross correctness for w not uniform: loops over all k with weight. Since w length 2n-1. For n up to 128, okay.
Potential performance: `for k, wk in enumerate(w): if wk == 0.0` but float exp underflow zeros. Good. Slices with reverse. Let's test slice logic with `stop` variable:
```
if start == stop:
   d[i_start] += wk*p[start]
elif stop == 0:
   d[i_start:i_end+1] += wk*p[start::-1]
else:
   d[i_start:i_end+1] += wk*p[start:stop-1:-1]
```
Example n=5,k=4: i_start=0,i_end=4, start=4,stop=0 -> p[4::-1] length5, d[0:5] ok.

`_conv_self` uses FFT for n > 200. For small search direct. But if we later search n up to 128 direct. For large-n fallback, could use FFT. We'll include threshold 512. For n up to 128 direct fine.
```
def _conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size < need: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:need]
```
Numpy convolve can handle arrays; direct C.

`_seed_p` for n maybe >? If we search up to 128, sine seed okay.
```
def _seed_p(n, idx, rng):
    if n == 1: return np.ones(1)
    kind = idx
    if kind == 0:
       p=np.ones(n); 
    elif kind == 1:
       x=np.arange(n)/n
       p=1.0 + 0.7*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    elif kind == 2:
       x=np.arange(n)/n
       p=1.0 + 0.5*np.tanh(3.0*(x-0.5))
    elif kind == 3:
       p=rng.dirichlet(0.25*np.ones(n))
    elif kind == 4:
       p=rng.dirichlet(0.75*np.ones(n))
    elif kind == 5:
       p=rng.dirichlet(1.5*np.ones(n))
    elif kind == 6:
       p=rng.dirichlet(3.0*np.ones(n))
    else:
       p=rng.random(n)
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-12)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=0: p=np.ones(n); s=1
    return p/s
```
Dirichlet with alpha small can produce near zeros; we floor to 1e-12 to avoid x log(0). But known opt likely positive.
Maybe add antisym seed: p=(1+0.7*sin) already.
Add "two humps" seed: p_i proportional exp(-((i/n - a)^2)/(2s^2)) + exp(-((i/n - b)^2)/(2s^2)) with a,b. Could help. We can in seed kind 7/8.
```
elif kind ==7:
   x=arange; p=np.exp(-8*(x-0.2)**2)+np.exp(-8*(x-0.8)**2)
elif kind==8:
   p=np.exp(-8*(x-0.35)**2)+0.5*np.exp(-4*(x-0.7)**2)
```

We need `cross_fft(p, w)`. w length 2n-1 dense. Implement with real FFT size L >= 3n-2. To save allocation, maybe use `np.fft.rfft` each iteration.
```
def _cross_fft(p, w, size):
    n=len(p)
    pr = p[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fw*fp, size)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Could precompute size based on n.

       theta_trial = theta - trial * g

       if score_trial < score + 1e-8 or score_trial < best*1.02: accept
   if none: restore, reduce lr

Full descent state:
```
def _large_n_descent(p0, n, deadline, max_iter=50000):
   p = p.copy(); p/=p.sum()
   theta = np.log(p)  # p positive
   # initialize moments zero
   c = _self_conv_fft(p,n); score=2*n*max(c); best=p,score
   lr=0.1; beta_factor=5.0; patience=0; no_improve=0
   while time +...:
      M=max(c); beta=beta_factor/M
      w = _soft_weights(c,beta)
      cross = _cross_fft(p,w,n)
      G = dot(w,c)
      grad = p*(2*cross) - 2*G*p
      g = grad - grad.mean()
      std = np.std(g)
      if std < 1e-12:
         g = rng.normal(n); std = np.std(g)
      g = g / std
      # Adam update
      ...
      theta_new = theta - delta
      p_new = softmax(theta_new)
      c_new = self_conv(p_new)
      score_new=2*n*max(c_new)
      if score_new <= score + 1e-9 or score_new < best*1.02:
          accept
      else revert, lr*=0.7
      if score_new < best: best... no_improve=0; beta_factor=min(100,beta_factor*1.02); lr=min(0.3,lr*1.01)
      else no_improve++; if no_improve>100: add random perturbation or reset to best
   return best
```

_G_BASIS_N = 0
_G_BASIS_SINES = None
def _basis_obj(x):
    n = _G_BASIS_N; S = _G_BASIS_SINES
    if time... raise
    if not finite x: return 1e100
    h = S.dot(x)  # scipy? use @
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if m > 0.95: h = h*(0.95/m)
    p = 1.0 + h
    # p should >=0.1; but due numeric clip
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
    p /= p.sum()
    return _score(p)
```

Direct active cross for large:
```
def _cross_active(p, w, nz):
    n=len(p); d=np.zeros(n)
    for k in nz:
       wk=w[k]
       if wk == 0: continue
       i_start = max(0, k-(n-1)); i_end=min(n-1,k)
       if i_start>i_end: continue
       start = k - i_start; stop = k - i_end
       if start == stop: d[i_start] += wk*p[start]
       elif stop == 0: d[i_start:i_end+1] += wk*p[start::-1]
       else: d[i_start:i_end+1] += wk*p[start:stop-1:-1]
    return d
```
For large n, if nz length <=8, direct. For k large, slices okay.
`_cross_general(p,w,n,fft_size)`:
```
nz = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-14)
if len(nz)==0: return zeros or max
if len(nz) <= 8: return _cross_active(...)
# FFT
pr=p[::-1]
fw=np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
fp=np.fft.rfft(pr, fft_size)
conv=np.fft.irfft(fw*fp, fft_size)
return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Soft weights function:
```
def _soft_weights(c, beta, active_tol=1e-300):
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 0: return uniform? 
    z = beta*(c-M)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    else: w=e/s
    return w
```
No need to return F.

Self conv FFT:
```
def _self_conv_fft(a, size):
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
   return c
```
Call with precomputed length 2n-1.

Softmax stable:
```
def _softmax(theta):
    theta = theta - np.max(theta)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 0.0)
    p=np.exp(theta)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(theta.size)/theta.size
    return p/s
```
Clipping to -50 means minimum p ~1.9e-22, sum unaffected for n6000 (1e-17). Good.
In large descent, after computing p_new, we can also set `theta = theta_new - theta_new.max()` to maintain.  return c
```

Let's implement `_score` robust:
```
def _score(p):
    a=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    a=a/S
    c=_conv_self(a)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return 2.0*len(a)*M
```
This matches evaluate (sum=1). For large p sum1.

`_basis_p(x,n,S)` with tanh:
```
h = S.dot(x)
h = 0.95 * np.tanh(h)
p = 1.0 + h
p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
s=p.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else p/=s
return p
```
If n global, S shape (n,K).
`_basis_obj(x)`: p=_basis_p(...); return _score(p). Add timeout.

DE bounds: `(-3.0,3.0)` for each coeff. Maybe some coefficients should larger? tanh saturates; 3 enough. Use setpole? no.
DE options: `maxiter=50`, `popsize=12`? Default 15. Use `tol=1e-5`, `atol=1e-8`, `polish=False` (we'll Nelder polish). If DE times out, catch.
Nelder starts: from best x found by DE (if any), from initial x0s. For each start maybe maxiter 200. But if DE already good, Nelder from DE best enough. We'll run Nelder from best_x and maybe x0_0.

Time partition in `_run_basis_search`: It receives `phase_deadline` (e.g., start+300). Inside use while time < phase_deadline-2.
Pseudo:
```
best_p = None; best_score=inf; best_x=None
x_starts = [...]
for x0 in x_starts:
   p=_basis_p(x0,...); sc=_score(p); update
# DE if time
if _HAS_DE and time < phase_deadline-30:
   try res=...
   consider res.x
# Nelder polish: list of starts [best_x, x_starts[0], x_starts[1]]
for x0 in unique:
   if time near break
   res=minimize(_basis_obj,x0,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':200,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-8})
   consider res.x
return best_p,best_score
```
If no scipy, just initial sine x0.

```

def _self_conv_arr(a, size):

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2 .

Strategy:
  - Keep a guaranteed small-length fallback.
  - Optimize a low-frequency anti-symmetric basis at a medium discrete length
    using differential evolution / Nelder-Mead when scipy is available.
  - Polish the resulting sequence with an FFT-accelerated log-domain gradient
    descent on a smoothed max-convolution objective.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    _HAS_DE = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_DE = False


class _SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global objective state
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_SEARCH_DEADLINE = 0.0
_G_BASIS_N = 0
_G_BASIS_K = 0
_G_BASIS_SINES = None


def _raise_if_deadline():
    if time.monotonic() >= _SEARCH_DEADLINE - 2.0:
        raise _SearchTimeout()


def _fft_size(need):
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    return size


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic scoring / numeric helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    size = _fft_size(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:need]
    return c


def _score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    c = _conv_self(p)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * p.size * m


def _sine_p(n, amp=1.0):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = 1.0 + amp * np.tanh(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    p = p / p.sum()
    return p


def _random_anti_p(n, rng, amp=0.75):
    h = np.asarray(rng.normal(size=n), dtype=np.float64)
    h = 0.5 * (h - h[::-1])
    h = amp * np.tanh(1.5 * h)
    p = 1.0 + h
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    p = p / p.sum()
    return p


def _softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.ones(theta.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(theta.size)
    theta = theta - np.max(theta)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(theta)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(theta.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(theta.size)
    return p / s


def _theta_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    th = np.log(p)
    th = th - np.max(th)
    return np.clip(th, -50.0, 0.0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Medium-length anti-symmetric basis
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _make_basis_sines(n, k):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    m = np.arange(1, k + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * np.outer(t, m))


def _basis_p(x, n, sines):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if sines is None or x.size != sines.shape[1]:
        return _sine_p(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return _sine_p(n)
    h = sines.dot(x)
    h = 0.95 * np.tanh(h)
    p = 1.0 + h
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return _sine_p(n)
    return p / s


def _basis_obj(x):
    _raise_if_deadline()
    n = _G_BASIS_N
    sines = _G_BASIS_SINES
    if sines is None:
        return 1e100
    p = _basis_p(x, n, sines)
    sc = _score(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e100
    return float(sc)


def _run_basis_search(n, phase_deadline, rng, current_best_score):
    global _G_BASIS_N, _G_BASIS_K, _G_BASIS_SINES, _SEARCH_DEADLINE

    k = 10
    _G_BASIS_N = n
    _G_BASIS_K = k
    _G_BASIS_SINES = _make_basis_sines(n, k)
    _SEARCH_DEADLINE = phase_deadline

    best_p = None
    best_sc = current_best_score
    best_x = np.zeros(k, dtype=np.float64)

    x_starts = []

    x0 = np.zeros(k); x0[0] = 1.0
    x_starts.append(x0)

    x1 = np.zeros(k); x1[0] = 0.85; x1[1] = 0.45
    x_starts.append(x1)

    x2 = np.zeros(k)
    for j in range(k):
        m = j + 1
        if m % 2 == 1:
            x2[j] = 1.0 / float(m)
    x_starts.append(x2)

    x3 = np.zeros(k); x3[0] = 0.6; x3[-1] = 0.35
    x_starts.append(x3)

    x4 = 0.25 * np.asarray(rng.normal(size=k), dtype=np.float64)
    x4[0] += 0.55
    x_starts.append(x4)

    x5 = 0.55 * np.asarray(rng.normal(size=k), dtype=np.float64)
    x5[0] += 0.35
    x_starts.append(x5)

    for x in x_starts:
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        p = _basis_p(x, n, _G_BASIS_SINES)
        sc = _score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_p = p
            best_x = x.copy()
            print(f"basis initial n={n} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    # Global low-dimensional optimization if available.
    if _HAS_DE and time.monotonic() < phase_deadline - 30.0:
        try:
            bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * k
            seed_int = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            res = differential_evolution(
                _basis_obj,
                bounds,
                maxiter=35,
                tol=1e-5,
                polish=False,
                seed=seed_int,
                update_interval=1,
            )
            if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                p = _basis_p(res.x, n, _G_BASIS_SINES)
                sc = _score(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_p = p
                    best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    print(f"basis DE n={n} score={best_sc:.6f}")
        except _SearchTimeout:
            pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Local polish with Nelder-Mead on a few promising points.
    if _HAS_SCIPY:
        polish_starts = [best_x]
        rel = []
        for x in x_starts:
            if not any(np.allclose(x, y, atol=1e-12) for y in rel):
                rel.append(x)
                polish_starts.append(x)
        if len(polish_starts) > 4:
            polish_starts = polish_starts[:4]

        for x0 in polish_starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= phase_deadline - 2.0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _basis_obj,
                    np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 180, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-8},
                )
                if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    p = _basis_p(res.x, n, _G_BASIS_SINES)
                    sc = _score(p)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = float(sc)
                        best_p = p
                        best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        print(f"basis NM n={n} score={best_sc:.6f}")
            except _SearchTimeout:
                break
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = _sine_p(n, 1.0)
        best_sc = _score(best_p)

    _G_BASIS_SINES = None
    return best_p, float(best_sc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full-sequence polish (FFT accelerated)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _self_conv_fft(a, size):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    need = 2 * a.size - 1
    return c[:need]


def _soft_weights(c, beta):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    z = beta * (c - m)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def _cross_active(p, w, nz):
    n = p.size
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk in nz:
        wk = float(w[kk])
        if wk == 0.0:
            continue
        i_start = max(0, kk - (n - 1))
        i_end = min(n - 1, kk)
        if i_start > i_end:
            continue
        start = kk - i_start
        stop = kk - i_end
        if start == stop:
            d[i_start] += wk * p[start]
        elif stop == 0:
            d[i_start:i_end + 1] += wk * p[start::-1]
        else:
            d[i_start:i_end + 1] += wk * p[start:stop - 1:-1]
    return d


def _cross_fft(p, w, size):
    n = p.size
    pr = p[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, size)
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _cross_general(p, w, cross_size):
    nz = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-15)
    if nz.size == 0:
        nz = np.array([int(np.argmax(w))], dtype=int)
    if nz.size <= 10:
        return _cross_active(p, w, nz)
    return _cross_fft(p, w, cross_size)


def _large_descent(p0, n, phase_deadline, rng):
    p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p0.size != n or p0.sum() < 1e-12:
        p0 = _sine_p(n, 1.0)
    p0 = np.maximum(p0, 1e-12)
    p = p0 / p0.sum()

    self_size = _fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    cross_size = _fft_size(3 * n - 2)

    c = _self_conv_fft(p, self_size)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    theta = _theta_from_p(p)

    lr = 0.12
    beta_factor = 8.0
    no_improve = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0

    print(f"large descent start n={n} score={best_score:.6f}")

    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline - 2.0:
        it += 1
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
            break
        beta = beta_factor / max(m, 1e-300)
        w = _soft_weights(c, beta)
        cross = _cross_general(p, w, cross_size)
        gdot = float(np.dot(w, c))

        grad = p * (2.0 * cross) - (2.0 * gdot) * p
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        g = grad - np.mean(grad)
        std = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
        if not np.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-12:
            g = np.asarray(rng.normal(size=n), dtype=np.float64)
            std = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
        if std <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(std):
            break
        g /= std

        theta_new = theta - lr * g
        p_new = _softmax(theta_new)
        c_new = _self_conv_fft(p_new, self_size)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c_new)):
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.5)
            continue
        c_new = np.maximum(c_new, 0.0)
        score_new = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c_new))
        if not np.isfinite(score_new):
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.5)
            continue

        improved_best = score_new < best_score
        accept = False
        if improved_best:
            accept = True
        elif score_new <= score + 1e-9:
            accept = True
        elif score_new <= score * 1.004:
            accept = True

        if accept:
            theta = _theta_from_p(p_new)
            p = p_new
            c = c_new
            score = score_new
            if improved_best:
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_score = float(score)
                no_improve = 0
                lr = min(0.35, lr * 1.035)
                beta_factor = min(300.0, beta_factor * 1.025)
                if it % 500 == 1:
                    print(f"large it={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={score:.6f} lr={lr:.3f} beta_factor={beta_factor:.1f}")
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.55)

        # Periodic escape mechanism.
        if no_improve > 450:
            restarts += 1
            no_improve = 0
            if restarts % 2 == 0:
                amp = min(0.12, 0.02 + 0.01 * restarts)
                theta = _theta_from_p(best_p) + amp * np.asarray(rng.normal(size=n), dtype=np.float64)
                theta = _theta_from_p(_softmax(theta))
                p = _softmax(theta)
                c = _self_conv_fft(p, self_size)
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
            else:
                p = _random_anti_p(n, rng, 0.75)
                theta = _theta_from_p(p)
                c = _self_conv_fft(p, self_size)
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
            lr = 0.14
            beta_factor = 10.0
            print(f"large restart #{restarts}, current={score:.6f}, best={best_score:.6f}")

    if score < best_score:
        best_p = p.copy()
        best_score = float(score)

    print(f"large descent finished n={n} best={best_score:.6f}")
    return best_p, float(best_score)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    global _SEARCH_DEADLINE

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    deadline = t0 + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Guaranteed small-length fallback, already below the trivial constant value 2.
    best_p = np.array([8.0, 4.0, 3.0], dtype=np.float64) / 15.0
    best_score = float(_score(best_p))
    print(f"initial fallback length={best_p.size} score={best_score:.6f}")

    # Optionally use the provided previous construction if it is already better.
    try:
        ref = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if ref.size > 0:
            sc_ref = float(_score(ref))
            if np.isfinite(sc_ref) and sc_ref < best_score:
                best_p = ref
                best_score = sc_ref
                print(f"using provided sequence length={ref.size} score={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    _SEARCH_DEADLINE = deadline

    # Low-dimensional medium-length construction.
    n_med = 4096
    basis_end = deadline - 900.0 if budget > 1400.0 else deadline - 60.0
    basis_end = max(t0 + 30.0, basis_end)

    basis_p, basis_score = _sine_p(n_med, 1.0), 2.0
    if time.monotonic() + 60.0 < deadline:
        try:
            basis_p, basis_score = _run_basis_search(
                n_med, min(basis_end, deadline), rng, best_score
            )
        except Exception:
            basis_p, basis_score = _sine_p(n_med, 1.0), 2.0
        if np.isfinite(basis_score) and basis_score < best_score:
            best_p = basis_p.copy()
            best_score = float(basis_score)
            print(f"best after basis length={best_p.size} score={best_score:.6f}")

    _SEARCH_DEADLINE = deadline

    # Choose a good full-sequence starting point.
    p_start = _sine_p(n_med, 1.0)
    if np.isfinite(basis_score) and basis_score < 2.0:
        p_start = basis_p

    # If the global best is already at this medium length, use it.
    if np.asarray(best_p).size == n_med and _score(best_p) < _score(p_start):
        p_start = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64)

    # Full-sequence polish.
    if time.monotonic() + 30.0 < deadline:
        try:
            desc_p, desc_score = _large_descent(p_start, n_med, deadline, rng)
            if np.isfinite(desc_score) and desc_score < best_score:
                best_p = desc_p.copy()
                best_score = float(desc_score)
                print(f"best after polish length={best_p.size} score={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final validation / cleanup against the evaluator's visible constraints.
    out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        # Absurd fallback, but keeps the evaluator happy.
        out = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = out / s

    # Do not exceed the time budget by returning only after time is safe.
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5:
        # The harness will kill later, but avoid printing if already over.
        return [float(x) for x in out]

    print(f"final returned length={out.size} score={_score(out):.6f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
